数据有三种格式，第一种
minecraft:lava_bucket 4
为该物品添加 4 点熔岩即可
第二种
额外：沙子、沙砾 2
是你要从已有的物品script\resource_locations\output里面遍历所有符合要求的物品/方块并添加
第三种
拥有以下方块数据
是我把要添加的物品都放在这些容器里了，从中提取物品并添加要素，要素的量你自己根据物品和该要素的关联度设定，要求最小为1，最大为16，平均为4

熔岩
minecraft:lava_bucket 4
minecraft:magma_block 2
cataclysm:lava_power_cell 8
kaleidoscope_nether:lava_roasted_chicken 4
spectrum:lava_sponge 6
spectrum:wet_lava_sponge 8
kaleidoscope_nether:lava_jelly 4
cataclysm:netherite_effigy 8
cataclysm:ignitium_helmet 8
cataclysm:ignitium_chestplate 8
cataclysm:ignitium_elytra_chestplate 16
cataclysm:ignitium_leggings 8
cataclysm:ignitium_boots 8
cataclysm:ignitium_ingot 4
cataclysm:ignitium_block 16
cataclysm:ignitium_upgrade_smithing_template 8
traveloptics:chronicles_of_the_firelord 16
spectrum:molten_rod 8
spectrum:blazing_crystal 8
spectrum:incandescent_essence 2
touhou_little_maid_spell:molten_fox_leaf 4
create_ore_excavation_plus:lavad_netherite_scrap 2

物质
ae2:matter_ball 4
ae2:singularity 16
ae2:quantum_entangled_singularity 8
mekmm:scrap 2
mekmm:scrap_box 8
mekmm:empty_crystal 16
mekmm:uu_matter 32
ae2:condenser 6

植被
额外：所有树叶，花，草，灌木，FID的所有果蔬 2

重力
spectrum:ring_of_denser_steps 16
spectrum:stratine_fragments 2
spectrum:stratine_gem 4
额外：沙子、沙砾 2
额外：铁砧 8
